# Themes index

Generated by `tools/build_themes.py` from the `- Themes:` lines of every survey entry (AAL/UUT year notes, drafts, pages, LW-only). Raw tags (1,600+ distinct, mostly singletons) stay in the per-unit notes. This file maps them into a curated vocabulary of top-level themes by regex, so an entry can appear under several themes. It is a finding aid for locating texts, not an interpretation; read the entries (and then the texts) before relying on a grouping. Edit the vocabulary in the script and re-run; do not hand-edit this file.

789 entries indexed; 3 carry no tag matching any theme (listed at the end). Dates are publication dates; drafts with placeholder header dates are shown as "undated".
